# STATE.json — Archived state_history

Cumulative, append-only archive of `.gsd/STATE.json`'s `state_history` entries older than the current + previous milestone (archive-by-milestone-boundary rule, mirrored in `.gsd/HARD_RULES.md`).

This file is never truncated or overwritten — only appended to, the same as `.gsd/archive/STEERING_LOG.md`, `CRITIC_REPORT.md`, and `VERIFICATION_REPORT.md` (rule 12).

---

Root cause: this session used PowerShell `Get-Content -Raw $path \| ConvertFrom-Json` (no explicit -Encoding) to read the file before every structural edit; Windows PowerShell 5.1 defaults Get-Content to the system ANSI codepage (Windows-1252) for a BOM-less file, misreading multi-byte UTF-8 sequences (em-dashes, curly quotes, degree symbols) as separate Latin-1/CP1252 characters, which then got re-serialized as valid-but-wrong UTF-8 on every single one of this session's ~15 writes -- compounding once per write, up to 13 layers deep on the oldest entries. This is a hard rule 14 violation this session itself caused, not one it merely failed to catch. Repaired via a Node.js script (bypassing PowerShell's Get-Content entirely) that, per string value independently (since corruption depth varies by how many writes happened after each entry was authored), repeatedly reversed one layer at a time: re-encode the string's codepoints as Windows-1252 bytes, strict-decode those bytes as UTF-8, keep the result only if that succeeds and differs from the input, stop (keeping the last successful result) on decode failure or a fixed point. 220 strings scanned, 29 changed (all in older, pre-this-session entries -- consistent with corruption depth decreasing for more recently authored text), up to 13 passes reversed per string. File shrank from 8,938,464 bytes to 101,250 bytes, all 68 state_history entries verified intact and reparsed as valid JSON, spot-checked em-dashes/degree-symbols now render correctly, no content invented or dropped (only byte-level re-decoding was reversed, semantics unchanged). A backup of the corrupted file was preserved outside the repo before repair. GOING FORWARD: this session will use Node.js (fs.readFileSync/writeFileSync with explicit 'utf8' encoding) for all further STATE.json structural edits instead of PowerShell's Get-Content/ConvertTo-Json/Set-Content pipeline, to prevent recurrence. Recommend a future rule addition to .gsd/HARD_RULES.md rule 17 or 14 requiring UTF-8-explicit tooling for JSON file edits on Windows specifically, since PowerShell 5.1's encoding defaults are the proven root cause here. |

Pre-flight (rule 10) clean: STATE.json valid JSON, no BOM, prior entry was a natural halt (state 4, antigravity-gemini, M29_P5 closed), .gsd/active/ empty and consistent with artifacts.active_spec=null. Stability check ALL FOUR GATES GREEN on the pre-existing tree: typecheck exit 0 (PASS 4/4 workspaces), build exit 0 (vite 8.2.0, 1887 modules), lint exit 0 (4 pre-existing only-export-components warnings), test exit 0 (1,970 passed / 2 skipped across 119 files: web 930/57, calculations 602+2/29, api 438/33). No Milestone 0 stabilization needed. codebase-mapper audited apps/web/src by reading source, not just grepping. Key findings: designSystem.ts already exists, is strictly constants-only, and is sound (26 exports) — it needs extension, not rebuild. Token adoption is lopsided: input 92/126, select 29/32, textarea 6/6, but button only 56/150 — 94 raw buttons across 40 files using 71 distinct class strings. Diagnosis of why patchwork persisted despite M23-28: this is not failed work, it is precisely the scope M24 explicitly deferred (ROADMAP.md line 340) and which the Deferred section (line 411) predicted would need scheduling 'if the drift keeps recurring'. Second mechanism: M24's AC-12 guardrail regex only bans local *named constants*, while the real drift lives in inline className literals and `${TOKEN} + override` template strings that the regex cannot see; and the token tests verify the vocabulary's contents but never its adoption — proven by BUTTON_ICON_CLASS and BODY_TEXT_CLASS having zero consumers while 15+ hand-rolled icon buttons exist. Modal.tsx (M25) is the counter-example that worked, because it pairs a component boundary with an adoption test (ScopeGuardrail AC-4). Confirmed defect: index.css sets a global :focus-visible 2px sky-400 (#38bdf8) ring, but INPUT_CLASS/INPUT_COMPACT_CLASS/FORM_SELECT_CLASS/FORM_SELECT_COMPACT_CLASS all start with focus:outline-none, which compiles to .focus\:outline-none:focus at specificity (0,2,0) and beats bare :focus-visible at (0,1,0) — suppressing the ring on ~165 token usages. Verified against the built CSS. Other findings: 112 <label> elements using 20 distinct class strings with no label token at all; 78 of 164 form controls carry neither id nor aria-label (32 in InventoryForm alone); ~63 numeric inputs expressed in 9+ class-string variants; components/calculators/CalculatorCard.tsx is a parallel primitive layer that re-hardcodes CARD_CLASS and SUBPANEL_CLASS byte-for-byte and uses `rounded` where INPUT_CLASS uses `rounded-lg`, so all 10 calculators render different corner radii; 17 tables in 5+ styles; ~14 ad-hoc badge styles outside STATUS_BADGE_CLASS. Migration hazards recorded for /plan: designSystem.test.ts pins every token's exact string; designTokens.test.ts AC-7 pins Object.keys length at exactly 26; AC-19/20/21 pin occurrence COUNTS (placeholder-slate-500=4, text-slate-600=13, border-slate-500/30=1) that any primitive migration breaks by construction; AC-13 pins 7 named files' token imports; designSystem.test.ts forbids the module exporting functions, so primitives must live in new modules; and no class-merging dep (clsx/tailwind-merge/cva) is installed. Findings handed to roadmapper, which appended the Living Design System initiative (Milestones 30-35) to .gsd/ROADMAP.md as six vertical slices sequenced by user-facing surface: M30 calculators hub + first primitives + focus-ring fix, M31 inventory/profile form label association, M32 recipe-designer numeric fields, M33 brew-day buttons, M34 dialogs and panels, M35 tables/badges + the adoption-guardrail rewrite. Verified by diff that existing M1-29 content is byte-identical; the single intentional edit is the 'Full-app design-token coverage' deferred bullet, now marked NOW SCHEDULED with its original text preserved verbatim. ROADMAP.md 415 -> 555 lines. HALTING per /map halt gate — awaiting user confirmation via /steer before any /plan begins. |

Root cause: the original per-file 'statically-unnamed control' counts were computed with a naive tag-boundary parser that treats the first literal '>' after <input/<select/<textarea as the end of the opening tag, which terminates falsely early on multi-line JSX tags containing arrow-function handlers (onChange={(e) => ...}), never seeing aria-label attributes declared after the handler and misclassifying fully-labeled controls as unnamed. The spec's own Resolved-Ambiguities prose methodology (brace/quote-aware boundaries, word-guarded id=, components/ui/** and *.test.tsx excluded, direct <FormField> children exempt) was always correct; only the applied count was wrong.
